#2463. Vendredi noir pour Stassin

Alors qu’il avait fait un appel du pied spectaculaire au sélectionneur la veille dans le quotidien belge Le Soir (« avec Saint-Etienne, j’évoluais en Ligue 2. Je n’avais donc pas grand-chose à revendiquer par rapport à mes concurrents. Désormais, je suis performant avec un club du haut du tableau en Espagne. Je pense donc avoir des raisons d’y croire (…) Marquer contre l’Italie et offrir la victoire à la Belgique a toujours été l’un de mes plus grands rêves.  »), Lucas Stassin n’a pas été convoqué chez les Diables Rouges par Mark Van Bommel hier. Ce dernier lui a préféré Hugo Cuypers, auteur de 6 pions en 13 matches cette saison avec Monterrey (Mexique).

L'avant-centre sous contrat avec l'ASSE jusqu'en 2029 et prêté cette saison au FC Séville pourra certes marquer contre les Italiens lors de cette trêve internationale mais seulement avec les Diablotins, qui défieront également l'Allemagne trois jours plus tôt (le vendredi 13). Un malheur n’arrivant jamais seul, le natif de Braine-le-Comte a dû se contenter d’un match nul (0-0) à domicile hier contre Alavès en ouverture de la 12e journée de la Liga. Et son compteur but reste bloqué à 1 en 7 matchs. Performant vraiment ?

#2462. UEFA Women's Champions League Preview: PSG Féminines vs. FC Bayern Frauen

PSG and FC Bayern will lock horns in their bid for a second UEFA Women's Champions League win of the campaign on Tuesday night. Both teams are unbeaten so far, but they have only won their matchday 2 fixtures, which left them outside automatic quarter-final spots.

Fresh from setting a new record of going 50 Frauen-Bundesliga games unbeaten, FC Bayern will look to extend their winning streak at PSG to three games. Like their last year's fixture that ended in a 3-1 win for Bayern, the game will take place at Parc des Princes.

PSG only finished with two points in the league phase last term, but they already returned home with a point from trips to Real Madrid and Manchester City this time around. And they recorded their first-ever league phase victory with a five-star performance against OH Leuven in the first day of October.

Bayern's solitary win came away from home, thanks to three late goals against Benfica in the middle of home draws with Manchester City and Real Madrid. Jose Barcala’s side squandered a two-goal lead to the WSL champions in the opening matchday, but they did the reverse against the Spanish club a fortnight ago.

While FC Bayern have won all of their first nine Frauen-Bundesliga games, PSG are already five points behind giants OL Lyonnes, who scored 48 goals in only eight Première Ligue games.

Center-back Vanessa Gilles and Kassandra Potsi didn't travel with the team after suffering injuries during FC Bayern's 3-1 win over Bremen. 19-year-old midfielder Sophie Proost is also unavailable because of illness. Regina van Eijk made her Bayern debut after Ena Mahmutovic's red card against RB Leipzig, but the number one will now regain her place for the big fixture.

As he usually does before European nights, Barcala rested key players on Saturday. Germany captain Guila Gwinn and Jovana Demnjanović are now back to full fitness, while Magdalena Eriksson, Momiko Tanikawa and Franziska Kett have all entered after the hour mark in a game the champions scored twice in the final five minutes to keep their perfect record intact.

As for the hosts, the captain Sakina Karchaoui, Anais Ebayilin and Rasheedat Ajibade are all out due to injuries.

PSG: Kiedrzynek; Isabela, Mbock ©, Samoura, Carmona; Yaya, Osteras, Echegini; Kangjinga, Feller, Elimbi

Bayern: Mahmutović; Gwinn, Eriksson ©, Ballisager, Kett; Amani, Tanikawa; Dallman, Harder, Bühl; Demnjanović

#2461. Access, Not Chances: How Como Took Manchester United Apart – LC

von Next Generation am 24.10.2026 in den Kategorien Champions League,Uncategorized mit 0 Kommentaren

Como beat Manchester United 4-1 — and in this case the scoreline really is a fair expression of the game. For long stretches the match was one-sided, not in terms of chance volume but in terms of access: Como dictated in every phase where the ball went, who received it, and under what conditions it had to be played on. United never got on terms in any of those phases.

Cesc Fàbregas’s side showed two things that rarely go together. Out of possession, a pressing structure with clear direction, isolation of one side, and man-marking that applied near the ball only for the action in progress while everything around it was already speculating on the second ball. And in possession, a positional game of remarkable technical maturity that ties opponents in, switches play, and works systematically into the space behind them. The 4-1 is therefore less an outlier than evidence of work that has been built up over recent seasons.

This analysis goes through the phases one by one: first Como in possession and United’s response, then the work against the ball from the high press to defending the box, and finally the goals.

In build-up, Como moved away from their nominal 4-2-3-1 and sat closer to a 4-3-3, with the eights as the real variable: very free in their movement and above all active in the half-spaces. The base structure stayed stable — a flat four plus a six in front of them, sometimes ahead of the line, sometimes slightly behind the forward line.

Close to their own goal, Como consistently involved goalkeeper Butez as an additional central player in the build-up. That created a five-against-four against United’s front line — awkward on paper, more awkward still in execution, because Como didn’t use the overload to play through but to tie opponents in. From there, the same shape kept appearing on both the right and the left: centre-back, full-back, a player from the half-position in the eight space, and a six alongside. A diamond that wasn’t an end in itself but had a purpose: the diagonal route out.

Como’s build-up structure. From a 4-1 (flat four), the half-positions with Paz and Perrone are added. Small triangles and diamonds emerge. The three attackers ahead of them create additional options in behind.

One detail deserves particular attention. At times the full-backs stood not wide but flat, in a half-position. That sounds like a nuance, but it had a clear function: it vacated the wing and created the space the eights could drop into — Paz and Perrone did so regularly. If you don’t occupy the wing yourself, you force the opponent to guard it anyway.

ManU got no access near the ball and left large diagonal spaces open on the far side.

For United’s midfielders this was close to unsolvable. Tielemans, Mount and Andrey Santos spent the whole game struggling to establish any responsibility at all, let alone get into duels. When an eight dropped out, they couldn’t follow, and if one of them was tied in by it, a two-against-one opened up on the far wing. Como didn’t create that situation once; they created it serially.

The decisive mechanism lay in receiving and relaying the ball. The player in the deeper position usually worked with two touches: the first took the ball backwards, the second relayed it to the free teammate. At the end of every one of those actions stood a player with the game in front of him — and from that open position he could switch play.

What made it possible were the preparatory movements. Paz and captain Da Cunha first lured their markers in the wrong direction and then worked with the head start they’d bought. That head start isn’t only space, it’s above all time: time to control the ball first and relay it second, rather than having to do both in one touch.

A scene in the 17th minute is typical. Mason Mount steps out, Mazraoui goes into the duel at the same time — nominally United are in an overload here. Only neither of them really gets to the opponent, because both are too late. Paz can therefore calmly take his touch backwards and switch play directly with the second, dissolving the pressure before it had even formed.

When the build-up ran down the right, it showed in a recurring pattern: Paz and Da Cunha took the first touch backwards or inside and then switched with the left foot out of a closed body position. The players they found were systematically in open positions — Milla, the far-side six, or the wing-to-wing connection directly.

In the second half, Como adjusted the shape and more often played with two sixes, to form the diamonds faster and switch to the far-side six faster. Couto stood out in this: the right-back repeatedly played the chip into the six or ten space — six or seven times in a row during one spell. It was mainly Paz who received the ball there and could launch the attack.

In the second half Como focus on those diagonal spaces and look for them mainly with chipped balls.

Especially striking inside the diamond are the rotations between the players. Because of the connections between them, they moved in rotation depending on running path, space and ball movement. The same recurring spaces were re-occupied. Under pressure on the ball carrier, the outer players pushed flat or ran in behind the opponents; the highest player in the diamond burst out of the cover shadow and became the reference point in depth. On a deep pass, the two wide players released and joined the depth, able to offer themselves again immediately in an open body position. If the pass went via the flat wide players, the spaces in front of the new ball carrier could be run into. Every rotation, every positional adjustment was coordinated with the others and, in the end, produced a stream of pressure-resolving passes while Manchester United chased the press in vain.

Como bridged the middle third exceptionally quickly, via dribbles at speed and diagonal passes. Behind it stood a recognisable principle: first tie the opponent to one wing, then get behind them via the diagonal, and in the final third do the same thing again. In sum, that produced the shape of a Z — build-up on the right, transition on the left, finish from the right again.

In this example Como pin ManU in the press with their short passing. Thanks to the extra attacker on the ball-near side they can play through the pressure and switch.

The connections between wingers and striker carried these attacks and were deliberately kept flexible. The wide players sometimes stayed wide, sometimes came short, sometimes tucked inside. Douvikas accordingly either waited deep in front of the line for the chipped ball or, when space opened up on the wing, received the deeper pass and then ran in behind along the wing as soon as the ball sat on the full-back’s open foot. That flexibility produced the overloads on one side, and the overloads produced the central overload.

In the final third one element was added that is rare in fast transition teams: patience. Como drew opponents towards them with dribbles and short passes, used back-passes when needed, and then switched in front of the block. Da Cunha, Baturina and Paz were the three who could tie opponents in and then find the diagonal. They created the positions on the diagonal, were available to receive, and used their space to apply pressure. The block was usually tied in with short touches, the switch and the position behind the opponent prepared, and quick finishes sought.

The intensity in possession shouldn’t be underestimated. Practically every attack involved at least three or four players, the width was occupied, the centre likewise, and there were multiple options throughout. What they looked for almost every time was the space behind the opponent on the far side — exactly the zone that is closed last when a block shifts across.

Paz brought the right profile for this pattern. A strong left foot cutting inside from the right, he repeatedly dropped into the right-hand blind side of his opponents, got shots away there with few touches, and racked up the most shots of the match. That the finishes generally came off few touches isn’t a detail but the consequence of the time won further back.

As the move continues, Como again look for the blind side of the defenders. Here Douvikas and Paz are positioned in behind, with Da Cunha joining in front of the back line. After briefly pinning the opponents, the switch reaches Da Cunha, who misses the target from distance.

What carries all of this is the technical execution. Soft passes, sharp ones when the moment called for it, consistently well timed and played into the right foot — for ninety minutes. Passing that secure at that tempo is what makes a variable concept actually retrievable.

United’s basic problem out of possession was the hybrid. They pressed half man-oriented, but with a plus-one in the last line — a construction that forces compromises in both directions. It demands access up front that it doesn’t fund at the back.

There were certainly moments when it worked. When Dalot pushed through into the press on the right and passed his man backwards, a clean man-orientation emerged all the way up to the front — and with it a clear assignment for Mount, out of which the ball could be won. But that stayed the exception. Most of the time Dalot sat too flat out of possession, so United couldn’t trigger the duels up front and went into the press a man short.

Mount was the most conspicuous case in this respect. He barely got into a duel across the whole match because the decision to step out always came too late. Against a team that works with preparatory movements, a late decision is practically the same as none: the opponent already has the head start before the journey even begins.

On top of that came a problem on the far side. There, United’s winger frequently failed to protect the depth — usually Mbeumo, who jogged back rather than closing the space. But that was precisely the target zone of Como’s diagonal switches. It allowed them to change sides almost at will and regularly find an open structure on the other side.

The second half brought no improvement. The distances to opponents stayed large, and centrally what was actually intended still didn’t materialise. Michael Carrick’s double change at half-time was meant to bring more physicality to the six with Carlos Baleba, but it didn’t solve the problem — the assignments still didn’t work, the duels in the centre still didn’t happen, and the wide positions dragged the eights out further, which opened new spaces.

In the end the energy simply wasn’t there to press with the necessary intensity. United’s press became increasingly static, and a static press against an opponent with this passing quality yields not a single ball win — it only hands them the starting positions they’re looking for anyway.

Como started the game in a high press and sustained it above all in the first half, with isolated spells after the break too. It was triggered on back-passes and goal kicks — and, remarkably, by their own goals: after every strike the team immediately pushed high again instead of managing the lead in a deeper block.

The steering ran consistently towards United’s left build-up side. The reason is personnel: Harry Maguire played there with his weaker left foot as the outside foot, and next to him stood another right-footer in Noussair Mazraoui on the left. Two right-footers on one build-up side means the ball always has to be taken back inside to play on — exactly where Como were closing the door. The press was usually triggered by the left-sided forward, with the ball ending up at either Maguire or Mazraoui.

Pressing structure after the change in the second half.

In the centre Como defended man-oriented, with both sixes pushing very high and taking flat passes into United’s midfielders out of the game. That removed the short route through the middle as an option and left the long ball. But the long ball triggered nothing for United: the second balls went to Como practically every time.

That isn’t chance, it’s running work. The sixes consistently tracked the negative pressing paths back, anticipated the long ball early, and were in front of the line before it arrived. Luis Milla covered the most ground of any player on the pitch in this pattern — recovery runs after being played past, doubling up on the ball, attacking the second ball. When the long ball came from Maguire or one of the full-backs, a six was regularly in front of the line with an intense sprint, sealing the centre, taking away the diagonal follow-up passes and attacking the loose balls.

Defending the long ball and extra support on the second ball.

Once the ball was on one side, that side was isolated. The near-side forward had access to the centre-back and dropped back a touch when the pass went to the full-back, so that he covered centre-back and six simultaneously and closed the square pass. The far-side forward marked the goalkeeper. That served two functions at once: it took away United’s escape route via the keeper along with the switch, and it kept a player in position for the moment of the turnover.

The double pivot worked in a deliberate half-position. The near-side six stayed tight to his man, the far-side one dropped slightly behind him — securing the space and at the same time establishing access to the far-side centre-back in United’s back three.

The last line defended largely man-for-man in the high press, and very tightly. But the decisive detail is the one that carries the whole concept: the man-marking near the ball applied only to the action in progress. Around it, players were already releasing from their assignments and orienting towards the second ball. That is only retrievable at very high intensity — and it’s the reason United’s forced long balls produced not a single usable moment.

When Como dropped a line, the man-orientation at the second height stayed exceptionally high. Here too the back-pass served as the trigger to push up as a unit and tip back into man-marking. If United were in a closed body position, the Comaschi had already pushed up far enough to attack the back-pass at the moment it was played — a pattern that led directly to the 3-0. The half-position was at work there again: closing down level with the centre-back while simultaneously prepared to push through onto the next player deeper.

In the first half Como still had problems assigning the half-positions against ManU’s 3-2-5. In this example Fernandes moves to the wing, Como fail to establish responsibility and access and are played through at mid-height.

Up to half-time, though, Como had a structural problem with this. The pure man-marking didn’t come off cleanly, because of the two sixes in front of the line, one should really have tucked in and taken over the half-position for the centre-backs. That didn’t happen consistently. The consequence: a centre-back stepped out, the space behind him stayed unoccupied, and that was exactly what United kept attacking.

The pattern of pressing onto Manchester United’s left side continued in the second half, now with Dalot at full-back. The press was still triggered by Douvikas, with the ball-near winger pushing up as a second striker to press the centre-back. Paz focused on the visitors’ ball-near six and Couto pushed through onto Dalot. Through the central connections the game was isolated on one side.

At half-time Como adjusted. One six moved directly into the line, with the far-side winger pushing in onto the six — they now pressed out of a 5-2-1-2. Lucas Da Cunha pressed through from the right onto Maguire, while Nico Paz picked up United’s six. The central forward pressed the central centre-back and from there carried on to the goalkeeper. The far-side wide midfielder and the remaining six were poised — one onto United’s free six, one onto the far-side centre-back — again in that half-position between opponent and the space to be protected.

ManU building up high against the man-oriented Italians.

The effect was immediately visible. Como controlled the half-positions, got access right into the last line throughout, and set the rhythm of the match from then on. The adjustment solved exactly the problem the first half had left open, without changing the underlying principle.

In the final phase Como defended from a very deep line inside the box. That left barely any space behind the line to play into — which United hardly looked for anyway. The visitors attacked little depth and rarely occupied the width in the final third, so Como could stay compact without being punished for it.

Accordingly, a lot of players stood very centrally in and in front of the box. That produced numerous cover shadows and a numerical superiority around the ball, which allowed them to steer actively within the block rather than merely react. At times they deliberately steered inside, to guide the opponent into the prepared congestion and cover from a deeper position.

Deep back line in the box, centre closed and play steered inside. The box defending allowed the English side shots from distance.

The crucial point is that Como left the gaps open not despite this set-up but because of it. If you pack the box and secure the depth, you give up the space in front of the line — and that is exactly where United were supposed to shoot from.

The numbers vindicate the decision. Taking the conceded goal out of the box, Como allowed nine shots from outside the penalty area across ninety minutes, worth roughly 0.24 xG combined; only three of them were even on target. On top of that, of the four shots inside the box, two came from at least 14 to 15 metres and effectively belong in the long-range category as well. United accepted the invitation, and the shooting quality was perfectly decent — it’s just that with that scoring probability over a whole match, the most likely outcome is precisely what happened.

1-0, Nico Paz (2′) — The goal comes before United are even set. Como take the goal kick quickly and play straight to the full-back, luring the visitors into the press — only not cleanly. Mason Mount triggers the pressure and is played past, and on the second ball he goes into the duel as well instead of holding the middle and looking for the space where he could pick the ball up. That is exactly what opens the centre, the space for the second ball is there, and Paz can play it through.

What follows is the transition principle in its purest form: a quick switch to the diagonal side, then short passes against a very deep line. Matheus Cunha doesn’t pick up the player behind him and leaves the ten space open, where Paz is standing. He needs two touches because neither Cunha from above nor Maguire from his distance gets into the duel. Lammens, for his part, doesn’t defend the goal with his cover shadow but takes the near post — which leaves the far corner to a strong left foot. The curled finish into it is quality, though.

2-0, Anastasios Douvikas (49′, assist Paz) — Same zone, same cause. Bryan Mbeumo doesn’t choose to shut the centre but closes the centre-back, which creates the space for Da Cunha, who drives forward and plays Martin Baturina in. Centrally, Mount again fails to pick up his man. Out wide, Amad Diallo, introduced at half-time, generates no pressure centrally, so Mazraoui can’t take over the deep wide player in the first place.

The result is Paz behind the opponent, in the ten space, dribbling unopposed at goal. Matthijs de Ligt establishes no reference to Douvikas’s run in behind and slips as well — a one-against-one with the goalkeeper that Douvikas finishes coolly.

3-0, Nico Paz (57′) — The only goal that comes directly out of the work against the ball. Como are in the midfield press in a closed position with access to the inside lane, win the ball there and punish a lapse of concentration in the visitors’ build-up. At the end, Paz is alone in front of Lammens again.

4-0, Jacobo Ramón (76′, assist Kaiki) — This goal comes from a longer possession spell and shows the assignment problem in its clearest form. Mazraoui is too narrow and has no access to the wide player. Diallo stands in the space with access to neither the central player nor the full-back nor the winger — an orientation that doesn’t work in any direction.

Como switch to Máximo Perrone, who can slide the ball out wide because the space is simply free. From there comes the cross from an open body position, and in the centre Como run a two-against-one at the end of which Ramón scores.

4-1, Joshua Zirkzee (90′) — Como’s only lapse, and one in a phase in which the team had already dropped very deep. United get the ball to the wing, it’s two against two in the box, and the cross is initially only feinted. Zirkzee orients himself well forwards at that moment, while Como’s defenders are already stepping out after the feinted cross. Diallo’s ball in behind the line is strong, the offside call is razor-thin in his favour, and Zirkzee takes two touches before finishing one-against-one with the goalkeeper. A blemish, not a structural problem.

The common denominator — Three of Como’s four goals come in the same zone: the space in front of and behind United’s line that nobody takes responsibility for. Mount is involved in two of those scenes through an assignment he never establishes, Diallo in two more after coming on. Nico Paz is at the heart of three of the four goals, twice as scorer, once as the provider — always out of the opponent’s blind side.

Como’s 4-1 has one common cause across every phase, and it lies less in any single idea than in its execution under continuous load. Out of possession they steered, isolated and speculated on the second ball — with man-marking that applied near the ball only for the action in progress and was already dissolved around it. In possession they tied opponents in, switched play and worked systematically into the space behind. Both require eleven players to anticipate a teammate’s decision, and both functioned over the full distance.

The connecting detail is the half-position. In the press, the far-side six stood between opponent and the space to be protected; in possession, the eights worked out of the same zone, and the full-backs occupied it flat in order to vacate the wing for exactly those players. Como, in other words, occupied the position in both directions from which two jobs can be done at once — which is why the side could neither be played past in the press nor be pinned in the build-up.

For United the diagnosis is more uncomfortable than the result. The problem wasn’t individual errors but that the team never, in any phase, got into the duels it needed. The mixture of half man-orientation and a plus-one in the last line funded no access up front, and without access every decision is made too late — from Mount in the centre to Mbeumo failing to secure the far-side depth. Carrick’s double change at half-time was meant to bring physicality to the six with Carlos Baleba, but it changed nothing about the assignments, and in Diallo a player came on wide who reached neither one opponent nor the other in his space. That the energy ran out in the end was a consequence rather than a cause.

That leaves the context. A 4-1 against Manchester United is no proof in itself, and Como’s finishing was exceptionally efficient on the night. What showed regardless of the result, though, is a team with a recognisable, rehearsed answer in all four phases of the game — and one that put a technical maturity on the pitch in possession that you wouldn’t readily credit to a side promoted two years ago.

#2459. Surveillez Houdayer !

Depuis leur écrasant succès (11-1) contre Ecotay-Moingt (R3) il y a un an en Coupe de France, jamais les Verts n'ont été donnés autant favoris avant une rencontre que ce soir contre Laval selon les bookmakers.

Ayant à coeur de se racheter après leur défaite à Guingamp, les leaders stéphanois ont remporté tous leurs matches à domicile cette saison tandis que les antépénultièmes Tango n'ont pris qu'un petit point hors de leurs bases.

Le Stade Lavallois, qui n'a jamais gagné à Geoffroy (mais y a subi 16 défaites et arraché 5 nuls), va débarquer diminué dans le Chaudron car il sera notamment privé de 3 avant-centres (tous blessés) : Corentin Fatou, Trévis Dago et Abou Kanté.

Mais les Mayennais ne seront pas complètement démunis offensivement. L'ASSE devra en particulier se méfier de leur meilleur buteur Mathys Houdayer (21 ans), qui a claqué 4 pions (contre Nantes, le Red Star, Pau et Boulogne).

"Artiste et artificier de ce début de saison tango" dixit Ouest-France il y a 8 jours, il a été désigné 6 fois meilleur joueur lavallois cette saison par l'entraîneur de l'équipe d'en face selon Foot National (seuls le Rémois Yahia Fofana et le Nancéien Ilyes Housni ont recueilli davantage de suffrages).

Passeur décisif lors des 2 matches de barrage contre Rouen en mai dernier, le natif de Laval, qui a prolongé son contrat avec son club formateur jusqu'en 2029, a mis en exergue sa polyvalence il y a 2 mois dans le quotidien régional le plus diffusé de France.

"Un attaquant, ça fonctionne avec la confiance. J’ai celle du coach, des supporters, de mes coéquipiers et de mon premier but contre Nantes. J’aime beaucoup être sur le côté. Mais en début de saison, le coach m’a dit qu’il essaierait de m’utiliser le plus possible en faux n°9, 9, 10. pourquoi pas 8 de temps en temps. En 10, je peux prendre des espaces, avoir des joueurs qui fixent les défenseurs, et me laissent le temps de me retourner. C’est un peu régal pour moi."

#2458. UEFA Women's Europa Cup Preview: Wolfsburg and Frankfurt eye quarter-final spots

After their head-to-head game in the Frauen-Bundesliga, Eintracht Frankfurt and VfL Wolfsburg will now hope to advance to the quarter-finals of the UEFA Women's Europa Cup. Wolfsburg won Friday's duel 3-1 away from home to open a seven-point gap over third-placed Frankfurt in the standings.

Stephan Lerch's side will host league leaders FC Bayern at the AOK Stadion in a decisive fixture next Saturday. Although the champions extended their unbeaten league run to 50 games over the weekend, the gap between the two giants is only three points.

However, Wolfsburg first have to deal with the return leg of the Round of 16 tie in the Europa Cup. Since they beat the Vrouwen Eredivisie side Feyenoord 7-0 a fortnight ago, the two-time Champions League winners will most likely rest key players on Tuesday.

Feyenoord only played one game since the first leg as they ended their five-game losing streak in all competitions with a narrow win over Den Haag. Wolfsburg scored 15 goals in their first three games of a competition that started last season.

Eintracht Frankfurt were semi-finalists in the inaugural tournament, and they will hope to get closer to that when they host PSV on Wednesday. First-half goals from Aussie international Hayley Raso and the captain Laura Freigang secured a 2-0 first leg in Eindhoven for Niko Arnautis’ team.

If they qualify for the last eight, Frankfurt will face the winners of the tie between Real Sociedad and Fenerbahçe. The Spanish won the first leg 2-0 before Wednesday's anticipated fixture in Istanbul as Fener aim to play in front of 47,000 fans.

Wolfsburg's next path will be either Rosenborg or Brøndby. The two Scandinavian teams finished the first leg 1-1 a couple of weeks ago. The only way Wolfsburg and Frankfurt will meet in the Women's football Europa League equivalent would be in the two-legged final.

Häcken won the inaugural competition with a 4-2 aggregate win over fellow Swedish side Hammarby.

#2457. U17 : On Aytone puis on bétonne

On a eu quelques contres sur lesquels on a mal joué les coups. On a beaucoup subi même en étant bien en place derrière. C'est dommage qu'on ait concédé autant d'occasions alors qu'on avait décidé de fermer un peu le jeu. Le résultat est bon, on a gagné. Mais en ce qui concerne le contenu de ce match, il n'y a pas grand chose de positif à retenir. On a décroché une victoire sans prendre de but avec beaucoup de U16 qui ne jouaient pas à leur poste donc ça fait quand même quelques satisfactions."

Après un premier quart d'heure plutôt équilibré, l'ASSE a pris l'ascendant mais n'a pas su ouvrir le score malgré deux grosses occasions de son très remuant ailier gauche Etienne Mendy et une tentative de Mamadou Konté sauvée in extremis sur sa ligne par un défenseur.

Sainté a concédé l'ouverture du score à la 56e minute suite à un 6 mètres joué trop vite suivi d'une perte de balle. Les Maralpins ont bien joué le coup sur cette action qui semblait quand même entachée d'une légère faute sur Mamadou Konté.

Boostés par l'apport de leurs entrants, les Verts ont égalisé à la 86e minute sur une ouverture de leur latéral droit Tom Teillol pour Lamine Sonko, qui a résisté au retour de deux défenseurs avant de mettre un petit piqué pour tromper le mal prénommé mais bien nommé Victor Battu.

A la 92e minute, Nathan Kasia a perdu le ballon mais s'est bagarré pour le récupérer et lancer Noah Moulin, dont le centre tendu a été repris victorieusement par le supersub Lamine Sonko. Ce succès permet aux Verts de monter provisoirement à la 2e place du classement, derrière le solide leader Berre.

La compo : Colombet - Teillol, Gueye, Kasia, Lenou - Depalle (Thouilleux, 82'), Rised, Reynaud (Cheikh, 75')- Toty, Konte (Sonko, 68'), Mendy 'Moulin, 68')

La réaction de Sylvain Gibert : "On a su faire la différence à la fin du match mais on aurait mérité de mener au score à la pause car la dernière demi-heure de la première période a été très bonne. On a manqué d'efficacité devant le but, c'est donc notre adversaire qui a ouvert le score. Mais on est reparti de l'avant, on a insisté et au final on a été récompensé. C'est la victoire de tout un groupe, les entrants notamment nous ont apporté l'énergie nécessaire pour renverser ce match. Les joueus étaient venus ici pour gagner et ils se sont donnés les moyens d'y parvenir. Il faut les féliciter pour ça. On est content d'avoir décroché ce succès sous les yeux de Christian Lopez, qui nous a fait l'honneur d'être présent et d'échanger avec nous à la fin du match. C'était top car c'est un joueur que j'ai cité la saison dernière dans mes causeries. Je trouve qu'il représente les valeurs du club, cette capacité à ne jamais renoncer, comme l'illustre son mythique intervention sur Oleg Blokhine contre Kiev."

#2454. Felix Nmecha breaks silence on controversial crown celebration

Germany sporting director Rudi Völler announced on Monday that he intends to hold talks with Borussia Dortmund midfielder Felix Nmecha following the controversy surrounding his goal celebration over the weekend.

After getting on the scoresheet in Dortmund's 2-1 win over SV Elversberg, Nmecha kneeled down, removed an imaginary crown and placed it on the ground – a celebration he had also performed after scoring for Germany in their 7-1 win over Curaçao at last summer's World Cup.

The celebration has been associated with the evangelical network “Ballers in God”, which LGBTQ+ organisations have described as “Christian fundamentalist”, and its campaign “The King's Return”.

According to research by, among others, ARD's Monitor programme, parts of the religious group hold homophobic and anti-LGBTQ+ views and describe abortion as “Satan's plan”.

However, Nmecha has now taken to Instagram to clarify the meaning behind his celebration, insisting that he was not seeking to promote any organisation, discrimination or hate, but rather to express his faith in Jesus.

“It has been brought to my attention that my recent goal celebration has led to some misunderstandings,” Nmecha wrote.

"My celebration is not intended to promote myself or any organization. Rather, it is about laying my "crown" before the soon coming King and pointing to Jesus, my hope and strength!

"My message has never been about promoting intolerance, discrimination, or hate in any form.

"As Christians, our message is one of love and respect for everyone, regardless of where they come from, what they believe, or what their worldview may be.

“For God so loved the world..”, John 3:16," he concluded.

Nmecha is part of Germany's squad for the upcoming fixtures against Serbia on November 13 and the Netherlands on November 16.

#2453. Sainté se reprend

Quelques minutes plus tard, c'est en transition que les Verts allaient frapper. Pedro récupérait un ballon devant sa surface et transmettait à Bernauer dont la passe cassait les lignes pour trouver Breum dans l'axe à 30m. Le Danois décalait Cardona qui ajustait le gardien lavallois (1-0, 45+1)

Les Verts avaient fait le plus dur au meilleur moment et allaient enfoncer le clou en début de deuxième période. En inversant les rôles : Cardona à la passe en centrant, Breum à la conclusion de la tête seul face au but à 3 mètres (2-0, 50e)

A partir de là, les Verts se contentèrent de gérer. Par deux fois, ils eurent l'occasion de tuer le match mais Davitashvili perdait son duel en frappant à côté du but et El Jamali se trouait de façon incompréhensible dans les 6 mètres alors qu'il n'avait plus qu'à conclure.

Pas de regrets toutefois pour les Verts qui profitent des revers de Pau, Montpellier et Reims pour reprendre un peu d'air au classement. Toujours en tête, ils devancent Nancy de 3 points avant de passer par la case Coupe de France.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Nelle ultime due sfide gli Azzurri si giocheranno la vittoria del girone. Dopo il via libera della Fifa al cambio di nazionalità sportiva, Palma ha ricevuto la sua prima convocazione nella Nazionale maggiore. Nel raduno figurano per la prima volta anche Cassano e Liberali del Como, Lontani del Parma e Cisse del Milan.

#2433. La situation se corse pour les U19

Mal en point(s) les U19 Stéphanois (7 points en 9 rencontres) se déplaçaient en Corse ce matin pour y affronter Bastia, 3 points de plus au compteur. Un peu rassurés après les promesses entrevues face à Monaco (1-2), les protégés de Frédéric Dugand ont à nouveau affiché leurs limites, s'inclinant à nouveau (c'est la 4ème fois) sur le score de 0-3 !

En première période, la possession est verte, mais de façon stérile. Les Stéphanois sont maladroits et rejoignent la mi-temps sans le moindre tir cadré ! En face, Bastia va de son côté être très réaliste et  tromper à 2 reprises, sur autant d’occasions, le portier vert Lorik Vial, aux 40ème et 44ème minutes. (Bastia 2  ASSE 0 ; mi-temps).

En seconde période les Verts ne sont pas plus inspirés, avec une seule occasion, une tentative de Fousseny Sidibé Kaloga bien stoppé par le gardien Bastiais. La fin de match tourne au cauchemar, avec un rouge récolté par ce même Fousseny Kaloga et un troisième pion encaissé sur la 3ème tentative corse (90ème) ! (Bastia 3  ASSE 0 ; score final).

Avec cette 5ème défaite consécutive (la 7ème au total), les jeunes Verts sont dorénavant avant derniers et relégables, seul Borgo (1 point) faisant moins bien qu’eux. Une réaction est attendue et obligatoire la semaine prchaine, avec la réception de l'US Cap d'Ail, 12 points, auteur d'un nul aujourd'hui face à Balma (2-2).

La Composition : Vial – Akil (Demassieux 70ème), E.Mnemoi, Assoumani (Lengi 46ème), Jolivet, Marliac, N.Mouton, O.Mendy, Kaloga, Zibi, Vibert (Gobé 62ème).

La réaction de Frédéric Dugand : « Il nous a manqué de tout. De la justesse technique, de la justesse dans nos choix défensifs comme offensifs. On ne peut pas rivaliser si on ne montre pas plus de caractère pour défendre et plus de lucidité devant le but. On a eu la possession mais quand tu ne cadres pas un tir..Notre production est insuffisante et la situation devient préoccupante »

#2432. Lang e la curva del Napoli: fischi dopo il pareggio con la Lazio

La frattura risale alla trasferta di Porto, quando l’olandese lasciò il campo senza salutare il settore ospiti.

La frattura tra Noa Lang e i tifosi del Napoli è emersa anche durante la partita contro la Lazio al Maradona. Secondo quanto riportato da TuttoMercatoWeb, L’esterno olandese ha cercato il sostegno della curva portandosi le mani alle orecchie, ma ha ricevuto fischi. La tensione potrebbe incidere sul suo futuro: a gennaio potrebbe tornare d’attualità una cessione.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il gesto con le mani alle orecchie sembrava rivolto ai sostenitori per chiedere di farsi sentire. La risposta, però, è stata una contestazione. Alla base del rapporto incrinato c’è quanto accaduto a Porto: dopo la sconfitta per 4-1, Lang aveva lasciato il campo senza salutare il settore ospiti.

Da quell’episodio la distanza tra il giocatore e una parte della tifoseria napoletana si è accentuata. Al Maradona, il tentativo dell’olandese di ottenere sostegno non ha cambiato il clima nei suoi confronti.

Sul campo Lang è stato tra i giocatori più vivaci del Napoli. Ha cercato l’uno contro uno, ha messo in difficoltà la Lazio e ha servito il cross da cui è nata l’autorete di Pedraza, valsa il pareggio azzurro.

Mentre i compagni festeggiavano, il classe ’99 ha indicato di nuovo l’orecchio alla Curva A. La contestazione è ripresa proprio durante una partita in cui la squadra di Allegri avrebbe avuto bisogno di compattezza. Al termine del match Lang è stato il primo ad abbandonare il terreno di gioco, accompagnato dal coro rivolto a lui: «Lang tu sei un figlio di pu…».

#2427. Christian Lopez : "J'ai vu des jeunes Verts entreprenants, déterminés, solidaires"

Christian Lopez revient pour nous sur le succès des réservistes stéphanois sur le terrain qui porte son nom.

Christian, tu as donné hier le coup d’envoi fictif du match que la réserve a remporté sur un terrain qui porte ton nom au Cannet-Rocheville, tout près de chez toi !

Effectivement, j’habite à 100 mètres du stade ! Depuis quelques années le terrain d’honneur porte mon nom et le terrain annexe porte le nom de Bruno Bellone, qui est issu lui aussi du Cannet-Rocheville. Bruno jouait ici avant d’intégrer le centre de formation de l’AS Monaco. C’est au Cannet-Rocheville que j'ai commencé à jouer au football à 10 ou 11 ans. A l’époque il y avait deux clubs, l'AS Rocheville et le Sporting Club du Cannet. Moi je jouais à l'AS Rocheville. Les deux clubs ont fusionné en mai 1968 pour devenir l'Entente Sportive du Cannet-Rocheville et j'en suis parti pour venir à Saint-Etienne, j'avais 16 ans.

Cela fait 27 ans que je suis revenu m’installer au Cannet. Pendant les 4 premières années, j'ai été au centre de formation de l'AS Cannes. J'ai été entraîneur là-bas, puis après entraîneur et directeur du centre. Quand l’AS Cannes a perdu le statut professionnel et donc le centre de formation, il ne pouvait pas me garder. A ce moment-là, comme j'avais sympathisé avec Madame la députée Michèle Tabarot et son frère Philippe (actuellement Ministre des Transports), je leur avais proposé d’intégrer le club et d’être intégré au service des sports de la mairie.

C’est ce qui s’est passé. J’exerce actuellement mon 2e mandat en tant qu’adjoint au maire du Cannet en tant que délégué aux Sports et à la Jeunesse. J’ai effectivement entraîné le club de la commune et j’en ai assuré la direction sportive. Je suis toujours membre du comité directeur de l’ES Cannet-Rocheville. J’ai été dans l’opérationnel mais j’ai maintenant 73 ans, à la retraite. Je suis bien sûr l’équipe première et assiste notamment à tous les matches à domicile. On me pose des questions sur le sportif, je donne mon avis.

Qu’as-tu pensé du match d’hier soir qui a opposé tes deux clubs de cœur ?

Déjà, bravo aux jeunes Verts qui ont su arracher la victoire ! Ce qui n'est pas bon pour nous, parce qu’on reste encore dans le bas du classement. J’ai vu une équipe stéphanoise où il y a de la qualité. Une équipe joueuse, qui ne se relâche pas. Ces jeunes Verts ne lâchent rien, j’aime cette mentalité ! Elle leur a permis de revenir au score et de prendre l'avantage dans les arrêts de jeu.

Le résultat reflète la physionomie du match ?

Je dirais que l’ASSE a su aller chercher ce succès. Un match nul n’aurait pas été illogique. On a eu le mérite d’ouvrir le score en seconde période mais ensuite on a été un peu naïfs, on a continué à attaquer comme si on avait un score à remonter alors qu'on menait. Il y a eu quelques oublis, des fautes de marquage et on l’a payé cash car il y a de la qualité en face. Je trouve qu’on a manqué de rigueur défensive. Même à 1-1, on a continué à attaquer alors qu'on était souvent pris sur des contre-attaques stéphanoises.

J'étais un peu énervé à la fin du match parce qu’on l’a perdu en encaissant deux buts dans les ultimes minutes. On n'a pas le droit parce qu'on a 3 ou 4 joueurs d'expérience dans notre équipe. On n'a pas le droit de partir à l'abordage quand on arrive vers la fin et qu'on sait que depuis le début de la saison, on perd des matchs dans les dernières minutes. À un moment donné, il devrait y avoir des joueurs qui prennent en main la direction de l'équipe. Il y a des postes clés dans une équipe. On est parti à l'abordage et on s’est fait prendre en contre.

Quels joueurs stéphanois t’ont tapé dans l’œil ?

Le numéro 5, Nathan Kasia, je l’ai trouvé très bon. C’est un jeune défenseur central de 17 ans qui me semble prometteur. Il a de la présence dans les duels, une bonne lecture du jeu et des qualités dans la relance. Le numéro 11 Etienne Mendy est un ailier gauche très percutant, très rapide, il a mis à rude épreuve notre défense. Comme il s’est beaucoup dépensé, il a été remplacé par le numéro 14, Noah Moulin, qui a fait une bonne entrée. C’est lui qui a centré sur le but victorieux d’un autre entrant, le numéro 15 Lamine Sonko.

Ce joueur nous aura fait très mal car c’est déjà lui qui avait égalisé quelques minutes auparavant. Il a mis deux vrais buts d’attaquant.  Au-delà des performances individuelles, j’ai apprécié les valeurs affichées par ce collectif stéphanois. J’ai vu des jeunes entreprenants, déterminés, solidaires. Il faut dire que ce sont des gamins qui s'entraînent tous les jours. Ils sont dans un centre de formation pour devenir des footballeurs professionnels. Nous on est dans le monde du foot amateur. Nos joueurs ne s'entraînent que trois fois dans la semaine.

A l’issue du match d’hier soir, l’entraîneur stéphanois Sylvain Gibert nous a confié qu’il avait été d’autant plus honoré de te rencontrer qu’il t’avait cité en exemple dans des causeries la saison dernière. Que tu représentais bien l’âme du club, cette capacité à ne jamais renoncer...

Cela me touche. Je l'ai vu avant la rencontre avec son staff dont Romain Hamouma et on a eu l'occasion d'échanger après le match. J'ai beaucoup aprécié ce moment. C'est toujours super d’être pris en exemple. Mais c’est aussi pour ça, ayant gardé l’âme d’un compétiteur, que ça m’agace vraiment qu’on ait perdu ce match comme on l’a perdu, alors qu’on menait encore à la 86e minute… Si j’avais joué, je ne les aurais pas pris ces buts ! On savait qu’il y avait des jeunes joueurs talentueux dans cette équipe stéphanoise, des garçons capables au dernier moment de te marquer un ou deux buts. Mais alors qu’on avait le match en main, on n’a pas réussi à les contenir !

Grâce à cette victoire arraché sur tes terres, la réserve stéphanoise s’est hissée à la 2e place. Quelques heures plus tard, l’équipe première a quant à elle consolidé son statut de leader de la Ligue 2 en dominant de faibles Lavallois.

Je n’ai pas vu le match mais je me réjouis que les Verts aient renoué avec le succès après leur faux pas à Guingamp. Cette victoire accrédite la thèse de Ian Cathro selon laquelle la lourde défaite au Roudourou n’était qu’un accident de parcours. Je suis plutôt optimiste quant à l’issue de cette saison. Sur ce que j’ai vu cette saison, l’équipe me paraît meilleure que celle de la saison passée. Je la trouve plus solide, plus solidaire. Les joueurs qui sont là jouent sérieusement. La saison dernière, sur le peu de matchs que j'ai vus, chaque fois qu’on perdait un ballon, je tremblais. Cette saison l’équipe dégage plus de sérénité, affiche davantage de maîtrise. J’espère évidemment que l’ASSE va poursuivre sur sa lancée et regagner sa place dans l’élite dans quelques mois.

11 novembre, jour de paix et de victoire. L'ASSE se devait de respecter la tradition...

#2426. Stuttgart lower asking price for Newcastle- and Galatasaray-linked attacker

After Bilal El Khannouss' loan move from Leicester City to VfB Stuttgart was made permanent for €15 million last summer, the 22-year-old could have left the Bundesliga club again almost immediately.

El Khannouss' contract with Stuttgart runs until the summer of 2030.

#2421. Les Amazones reçues 5 sur 5

Désormais seules en tête de l'Arkema Seconde Ligue, les Amazones se déplaçaient ce dimanche à Lille où elles se sont imposées 1-0 grâce à une Ithaisa Vinoly impériale dans les buts et un but tardif d'Héloïse Mansuy. Un cinquième succès consécutif en championnat qui permet à l'ASSE de conforter sa place de leader et de semer la concurrence !Après leur victoire convaincante face à Auxerre (2-0), les Vertes faisaient face dans le Nord à un concurrent sérieux dans la course à la montée et Yannick Chandioux devait en plus composer sans Rachel Corboz et Agathe Felden, absentes surprises de dernière minute.Sur la pelouse synthétique du LOSC, ce qui avait obligé les Stéphanoises à s'entraîner toute la semaine sur une surface identique à Saint-Priest-en-Jarez, les Vertes ne connaissaient pourtant pas l'entame espérée, les Lilloises imposant rapidement leur rythme et se montrant particulièrement agressives dans les duels. Dès la 9e minute, la meilleure buteuse de D2, Coralie Monguillon se présentait face à Ithaisa Vinoly, mais la gardienne espagnole remportait son face-à-face et préservait les cages stéphanoises.Le LOSC continuait de pousser et Wissem Bouzid voyait ensuite une nouvelle tentative dangereuse contrée in extremis avant que Chloé Neller ne trouve le poteau à quelques minutes de la pause. Côté stéphanois, seule Sarah Cambot parvenait à répondre sur une belle reprise de volée qui frôlait le montant adverse. A la pause, les Vertes pouvaient s'estimer heureuses de ne pas être menées.Mais au retour des vestiaires, les Amazones affichaient de meilleures intentions et se montraient davantage entreprenantes, même si les Lilloises continuaient de poser de sérieux problèmes à l'arrière-garde verte. À l'heure de jeu, un énorme cafouillage dans la surface stéphanoise faisait même passer un sérieux frisson dans le camp forézien avant de finir en corner.Les minutes défilaient et les joueuses de Yannick Chandioux peinaient toujours à prendre véritablement le contrôle de la rencontre. À la 74e minute, Coralie Monguillon se heurtait encore à une Ithaisa Vinoly exceptionnelle. La portière espagnole réalisait une parade déterminante et permettait aux siennes de rester dans le match.Et ce ne fut pas en vain car à 10 minutes de la fin du match, sur un coup de pied arrêté, un ballon traînait dans les cinq mètres cinquante nordistes et Héloïse Mansuy surgissait pour le pousser au fond des filets. Un avantage aussi précieux qu'inattendu (0-1, 80e) !Les dernières minutes devenaient alors particulièrement tendues, les Lilloises vexées tentant de revenir au score par tous les moyens possibles, faisant progressivement monter la nervosité entre les deux bancs.Sans surprise, la tension dégénéra et entraîna une pluie de cartons à la 90e minute, l'entraîneur lillois Mathieu Robail et la buteuse Coralie Monguillon étant ainsi expulsés mais malgré cette fin de rencontre particulièrement agitée, les Vertes ne lâchaient rien et conservaient leur avantage jusqu'au coup de sifflet final.Un précieux succès, acquis dans la douleur, qui permettait aux Amazones d'enchaîner une cinquième victoire consécutive en championnat et de prolonger leur invincibilité.Yannick Chandioux: "On retiendra avant tout la victoire. Et celle-ci, on la doit en grande partie à notre gardienne, Ithaïsa. Elle nous a maintenues dans le match pendant longtemps, alors que nous sommes passées à côté de notre entame, quasiment dès les premières minutes. Nous avons été moins agressives que les Lilloises, qui ont pleinement joué sur cet aspect et nous ont mises en difficulté (...) Ne pas avoir encaissé de but dans cette rencontre relève presque du petit hold-up. (...) Parfois, les saisons réussies se construisent aussi dans ce genre de matchs, que l'on parvient à gagner alors que peu de monde nous voyait l'emporter".Avec 17 points au compteur, les Stéphanoises conservent bien sûr leur fauteuil de leader avec deux longueurs d'avance sur Le Mans et trois sur Roubaix. Elles retrouveront leur public samedi prochain au stade Salif-Keita face à Nice, l'actuel 10e... d'un championnat à 11

#2420. Contribute to help victims of the Pedrógão Grande tragedy

Forest fires occur every summer in Portugal but the tragedy that struck the community of Pedrógão Grande in the centre of the country is unprecedented. Over 60 people lost their lives, many trapped in their cars when trying to flee from the flames.

This Just Giving page is a simple way for people, wherever they are in the world, to make a contribution to help rebuild these communities and bring some comfort to those who lost everything in the blink of an eye. The money raised will be sent to a network of local authorities and charities working in the area affected. Thank you for your support.

#2419. Vincent Kompany returns to Bayern Munich training ground after illness

Bayern Munich head coach Vincent Kompany was reportedly back at the club's training ground at Säbener Straße on Tuesday after being absent due to illness since collapsing on October 23.

According to TZ, Kompany was sitting at his computer in his office at the club's headquarters shortly after 10am.

The 40-year-old Belgian coach was greeted by assistant Floribert Ngalula and later held a longer conversation with sporting director Christoph Freund.

From his office, Kompany was also able to watch injured players such as Luis Diaz and Jamal Musiala continue their rehabilitation training on the pitch.

Kompany had been absent since collapsing on the training pitch on October 23, two days before Bayern Munich's 3-1 Bundesliga win over SC Freiburg.

The 40-year-old was subsequently diagnosed with meningeal irritation and continued his recovery at home after being discharged from hospital.

Bayern assistant Aaron Danks was in charge for the five games Kompany missed.

Kompany is expected to return to the touchline when Bayern Munich face 1. FC Köln in the Bundesliga on November 20, following the international break.

#2417. PSV hold on to book narrow Champions League win in Porto

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

PSV survived a second-half onslaught in Porto to secure their first win of the Champions League group stages.

Guus Til scored the only goal when he tapped in from close range after Esmir Bajraktarevic’s shot had come back off the post and was steered across goal by Sven Mijnans.

The Eindhoven club were worth their narrow lead against Francesco Farioli’s side after dominating possession in the first half, but rarely troubled Diogo Costa in the Porto goal.

Til went close with a header early in the second half before putting PSV ahead in the 57th minute.

But the visitors had to defend heroically as Porto poured forward in search of an equaliser, aided by an increasingly fragile Matej Kovar.

The goalkeeper put his defenders in jeopardy with a weak clearance in the 72nd minute, but redeemed himself by saving Victor Froholdt’s shot with his chest before Lutsarel Geertruida blocked the follow-up from Pepê.

A minute later Kovar was left stranded after he rushed out of his penalty area, but Sergiño Dest managed to hack the ball away before Alberto Costa could poke it into the open goal.

At the other end, substitute Denis Man came close to scoring a second for PSV with his first touch when his curling shot beat Costa but failed to sneak inside the post.

The 1-0 win lifts PSV to a provisional 16th place in the Champions League table at the halfway stage of round three, while Porto are still looking for their first point.

Their next match is at home to Belgian side Club Brugge, who will make the short trip to Eindhoven on November 4.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

La comunicazione del club rimanda alle indicazioni dedicate agli omaggi Under 14.

I bambini nati dal 1° gennaio 2022, indicati nella comunicazione come bambini di quattro anni, entrano gratuitamente senza biglietto presentando un documento d’identità o la tessera sanitaria. All’ingresso l’identità sarà controllata per ogni spettatore: anche i minorenni devono esibire un documento.

#2412. Bundesliga and NFL extend partnership to strengthen global collaboration

Having first entered into a partnership in 2022, the Bundesliga and the National Football League (NFL) have extended their Memorandum of Understanding (MoU), reaffirming their commitment to working together on cross-sport initiatives and creating new fan experiences worldwide.

The extension will see the two leagues continue to collaborate in key areas such as innovation, digital and experiential marketing as well as cross-sport content opportunities, while sharing best practices and exploring new ways to engage fans globally.

"The renewal of our MOU with the NFL is a pivotal step in the Bundesliga’s continued expansion in the United States,” said Bundesliga CEO Steffen Merkel.

“This collaboration not only deepens the connection between the NFL and Bundesliga but also plays a key role in our overall growth strategy in the US market.”

As part of the partnership, the NFL has staged five regular-season games in German football stadiums over the past four years, with matches taking place in Munich (FC Bayern München), Frankfurt (Eintracht Frankfurt) and Berlin (Hertha BSC).

In the United States, the Bundesliga and the NFL have collaborated on a number of physical and digital events. For example, former Bayern Munich star Rafinha joined ex-New England Patriots players Brian Hoyer and Sebastian Vollmer for a watch party ahead of the Patriots' home game against the Buffalo Bills in December.

Elsewhere, Borussia Dortmund legend Patrick Owomoyela announced the Pittsburgh Steelers' sixth-round selection at the 2026 NFL Draft in the Steel City.

“The partnership between the NFL and the Bundesliga demonstrates the value of bringing two of the world’s leading sports leagues together,” explained NFL Commissioner Roger Goodell.

“By extending our collaboration, we can continue to learn from each other, create new experiences for fans and explore innovative ways to grow our sports in Germany, the United States and around the world.”

Other initiatives have included NFL legend Adam Vinatieri setting an official Guinness World Record by kicking a field goal in the Swiss Alps at an altitude of 3,454 metres, with former Bayern Munich player Giovane Elber serving as the holder.

The Bundesliga has also been represented at the last two Super Bowls, with former players Claudio Pizarro and Roman Weidenfeller attending the events in New Orleans and San Francisco.

The Bundesliga's long-term US growth strategy is supported by a 17-year partnership with Relevent Sports. The agreement includes media rights, marketing and events, regional sponsorships, localised content and grassroots initiatives.

#2409. Top five football betting tips and tricks

Football is the world’s most famous sport, and it is the sport of choice of a great many sports punters as well. Especially popular in Europe, betting on football can be an incredibly profitable enterprise, so long as you know how to do it.

Getting started on football betting is far from easy, though. This form of gambling requires dedication, investment, and persistence. As you gain experience, your intuition will improve and you will be able to make more correct predictions.

But experience is not the only thing that can help you become a better football punter. In fact, you can substantially improve your betting strategy by simply following some basic football betting tips.

You might find shopping around boring and unhelpful, but you just have to do it when you search sites for football betting. As you know, the higher the odds, the bigger the payout. What you may have missed, however, is that odds are different at each bookmaker. Having said that, shopping around for the best odds is sensible, as it rewards you with bigger payouts.

In this line of thought, sticking to one bookmaker is virtually pointless. Even if the bookie of your choosing offers the best odds for a number of matches, it is highly unlikely that their odds will remain the best for long.

An obvious one, we know. And yet, you will be surprised by the number of football bettors who wager on football, but do not follow the sport. So, why is it important to follow the action closely?

First of all, by watching football matches, you can take part in the most exciting football betting market – the live betting market. What is more important, however, is that following the action provides an invaluable insight into teams and players’ style, condition, and determination – important factors  to consider when placing a bet.

As you watch more and more games, you will improve your analytic abilities. In time, you will be able to make more accurate predictions about the outcome of different matches, which, of course,  will result in placing more winning bets.

As you know, odds are based on the probability of occurrence of a certain outcome; the higher the odds, the more unlikely it is for that outcome to actually occur. But what happens when the odds offered by the bookmaker represent a probability that is lower than the actual probability of an outcome occurring? In such a situation, you can place what is called a value bet.

If you want to become a professional football punter, you should always search for value when betting, as it leads to huge profits

Favoured by beginners in particular, accumulators are a type of football wager which allows you to bet on as many fixtures as you like. Most commonly, you will be asked to choose a match winner, but as the market is diverse, you may be offered a number of other betting options. What makes accumulators an appealing betting market is the huge potential payout – here, you can stake as little as £1 and have the potential to win more than £500,000, depending on the bookmaker and the number of selected fixtures.

If you find accumulators’ payouts too good to be true, you are right. As a matter of fact, there is a reason for the odds to be set so high – actually winning an accumulator bet, especially one which involves a wide selection of matches, is nearly impossible. You see, all you need for your whole wager to lose is one wrong prediction. For example, if you bet on 10 matches and just one of them goes wrong, your entire accumulator is lost.

To make money by betting on football, avoid betting on accumulators – the risk is simply not worth it.

Finally, if you want to be a successful football punter, learn how not to mix emotions with betting. This is easier said than done, though.

Whenever your favourite team plays, you want them to win, regardless of how (im)possible that may be. If you do not learn how to leave emotions out of betting, however, you are highly likely to put money on your favourite team even if they have no chance of winning the match. Conversely, should you base your bets on hard facts and not on emotions, you increase your chances to win.

Our advice – avoid betting on fixtures that involve one of your favourite teams, that is the easiest way to keep emotions at bay.

The 66-year-old, whose current deal expires in 2028, said on the “Kerners 11” podcast:

Tavares salterà i prossimi due impegni del Portogallo: la sfida del 14 novembre contro la Danimarca e quella del 17 novembre contro il Galles. Il forfait del biancoceleste ha reso necessario un cambio nell’elenco dei convocati.

Al posto di Tavares, Jorge Jesus ha scelto Flavio Nazinho, terzino classe 2003 dell’AS Monaco. Per il ventitreenne si tratta della prima convocazione con la nazionale maggiore portoghese.

Arrivato nel Principato dal Cercle Brugge durante l’ultima estate, Nazinho si è imposto rapidamente tra le sorprese di questo inizio di stagione in Ligue 1.

#2402. U15 : de la joie contre les Caladois

Les U15 de Benjamin Petit pouvaient être heureux à la fin de leur match contre les Caladois du FC Villefranche Beaujolais. A l'issue d'une rencontre très disputée, comme souvent dans cet exigeant championnat, ils n'ont rien lâché face à un adversaire de valeur et ont su trouver les ressources pour l'emporter. Cela récompense leur volonté d'aller de l'avant, leur ténacité et leur concentration dans l'aspect défensif. Sans quelques joueurs retenus avec les U16 de Kevin De Jesus (Ilan Clapeyron, Riad Moutae, Kaïs Boukerma et Hugo Durin) tous ont su se mettre au diapason, et cette victoire est celle d'un collectif très solidaire.

L'entame du match a été stéphanoise, les Verts s'installant dans le camp caladois. Dès la 9ème minute, sur un centre venu de la gauche, Nolan Lebreton remettait pour Charly Davignon qui était devancé de peu par un défenseur. Deux minutes plus tard, un coup-franc de ce même Nolan Lebreton était capté par le gardien. Les Verts monopolisaient le ballon mais le dernier geste, souvent dans les petits espaces, manquait de justesse pour mettre de conclure. Peu à peu, Villefranche desserrait l'étreinte stéphanoise et se montrait dangereux sur coup de pied arrêté, mais Manoa Frison faisait bonne garde, notamment, à la 26ème, où il réalisait une double parade avant que Victor Hamouma ne s'interpose pour stopper la tentative adverse. Quelques minutes plus tard, Charly Davignon, qui repiquait au centre, était victime d'une faute laquelle donnait lieu à un coup-franc. Nolhan Fernandes le frappait et mettait le gardien à contribution. Peu après sur un long centre de Nolhan Fernandes, Hugo Bastide obtenait un corner. Ce corner, joué à deux, permettait à Nolan Lebreton d'adresser un long centre au deuxième poteau que le portier caladois dégageait. Les contres de Villefranche butaient sur une solide défense stéphanoise, concentrée et rigoureuse qui gérait bien les espaces et s'attachait à ressortir proprement les ballons. Le score était donc nul et vierge au repos.

La deuxième mi-temps repartait sur les mêmes bases, avec une maitrîse verte retrouvée et des situations intéressantes, sans parvenir toutefois à faire la différence.  Mais les Verts, suite à un carton blanc à la 54ème minute, se retrouvaient réduits à 10 pendant une dizaine de minutes, et ce fait de jeu freinait un peu leur dynamique. Ils allaient trouver la faille suite à un cafouillage dans la surface, avec une main adverse et un accrochage sur Mathis La Rosa. Toujours est-il que l'arbitre sanctionnait cette action d'un penalty que Charly Davignon transformait, donnant l'avantage aux Verts (68ème). La fin du match allait être plutôt hâchée avec plusieurs coups-de-pied arrêtés que Villefranche jouait directement. Les Verts se créaient cependant quelques opportunités mais un manque de justesse ne leur permettait pas de les concrétiser. Le match se terminait donc sur cette victoire stéphanoise somme toute logique, tant elle avait été bien construite.

La composition : Frison - Bergougnoux (Uneau, 50ème), L. La Rosa, Hamouma, Bastide - Wojcik (Davignon, 50ème), M.La Rosa, Lebreton - Fernandes (Madjidi, 50ème), Maganga Verrier, Davignon (Linganzi, 41ème).

La réaction de Benjamin Petit : "Le match a été serré, très disputé face à une bonne équipe de Villefranche, comme souvent dans ce championnat. Il n'y a pas de match facile et c'est un bon apprentissage pour les jeunes. Nous avons fait vingt premières minutes plutôt intéressantes, avec de la maîtrise, une volonté de défendre en avançant, un point que travaillons. Nous avons baissé en fin de première mi-temps, avec plus de difficulté à maintenir notre pression, ce qui nous a menés à défendre plus bas. En conclusion, je dirais que globalement, nous avons eu la maîtrise, mais mon regret est le manque de justesse, de percussion et d'efficacité pour créer du danger sur le but adverse et se mettre à l'abri. Je retiens notre solidité défensive, notre rigueur dans les prises en charge, notre détermination à ne rien encaisser, notamment sur les coups-de-pied arrêtés, un domaine dans lequel nous avons progressé. Il faudra néanmoins éviter certaines fautes, évitables, qui offrent des situations dangereuses à l'adversaire. C'était un match intéressant dans l'apprentissage, la gestion des temps forts et des temps faibles. Il faut toutefois  essayer d'être plus efficace dans nos temps forts tout en restant solides dans les temps faibles."

#2399. Sports club members becoming less tolerant of homosexuality

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Acceptance of homosexuality in Dutch sports clubs has declined significantly in the last generation, according to a survey of attitudes by the Mulier Institute.

The organisation found that 66% of people had no objection to a fellow member of a sports club being gay, while in 2008 the figure was 85%.

Just over half (55%) of respondents said they had no problem sharing a shower with a gay team-mate of the same sex, which also represented a drop from 73% in 2008.

The Mulier Institute said the findings reflected a social trend that has also been seen in schools, where the proportion of boys aged between 12 and 16 with a positive view of homosexuality has dropped from 40% to 22% since 2021.

The Dutch Olympic Committee NOC*NSF said the findings were “highly regrettable” and pledged to step up its efforts to make sport more inclusive.

But the John Blankenstein Foundation, which campaigns for LGBT emancipation in sport, said it had noticed declining enthusiasm for its workshops and growing numbers of parents who voiced objections.

Karin Blankenstein, who set up the organisation in 2008, told NOS: “I get the sensitivity around transgender people in sport at the top level, but this is about amateur sport.”

#2397. "We hope he makes the right choice" – Mancini on Tresoldi's decision between Germany and Italy

Eligible to represent both countries, Nicolo Tresoldi remains undecided over whether to play for Germany or Italy at senior international level.

The 22-year-old German U21 international has so far made himself unavailable for selection by either senior national team as he carefully considers which country to represent.

As Italy head coach Roberto Mancini revealed at a press conference on Monday, he and technical director Claudio Ranieri have both held talks with Tresoldi, hoping the Club Brugge forward will ultimately choose the Azzurri.

"I spoke to Tresoldi, and Ranieri did too. But I believe he must feel that he wants to play for the national team," Mancini said, via Football Italia.

"His father is Italian; he won the Euros with the U21s. I think there's a chance. He knows he can be an important player for the future; we hope he makes the right choice."

Tresoldi is currently with the German U21 national team, preparing for their upcoming games against Belgium and Croatia.

#2396. Les U16 n'ont pas fait de détail

Après leur déconvenue d'il y a trois semaines, et la défaite face au CASCOL Oullins à l'extrême limite du temps additionnel (3-2), les U16 de Kevin De Jesus avaient à coeur de réagir. La trêve avait été meublée par une rencontre amicale à Bourg-Peronnas remportée par 3 à 0 (buts de N.Fontaine et Joris Rochette 2), histoire de garder le rythme et de parfaire les automatismes. Les Verts se présentaient hier, sur le terrain synthétique Georges Bereta, avec une équipe dans laquelle figuraient quatre U15 (Ilan Clapeyron, Riad Moutae, Kaïs Boukerma et Hugo Durin) face à la lanterne rouge, l'AFCA (Aurillac Foot Cantal Auvergne) qui, il faut bien l'avouer, n'a pas opposé une farouche résistance aux Verts. Ces derniers l'ont en effet emporté sur le large score de 9 à 0, alors qi'ils menaient déjà 4-0 au repos.

Ce match a ressemblé à une attaque-défense, digne d'un match de handball. Dès la 2ème minute, Hugo Durin se mettait déjà en évidence avec un tir au ras du poteau. Dans la foulée, à la réception d'un ballon venu de la droite, il éliminait son adversaire et marquait  d'un tir au ras du sol. (ASSE-AFCA : 1-0, 3ème).Le jeu se déroulait devant le but aurillacois et Rayan Fontaine, servi par Adem Moussaoui, d'un tir dans un angle fermé aggravait le score. (ASSE-AFCA : 2-0, 7ème)La domination verte ne se relâchait pas : sur un corner, la reprise de la tête de Raphaël Moser était détournée par le gardien (17ème), un coup-franc de Lenny Nebout, tiré à ras de terre, était repoussé par le portier cantalou et la reprise de Noah Fontaine ne trouvait pas le cadre (18ème), un tir de loin de Dixon Ahamadi était une nouvelle fois détourné (22ème). Entre temps, une première incursion aurillacoise était ponctuée par un corner, mais la reprise de la tête n,'inquiétait pas Ilan Clapeyron.  Les Verts allaient de nouveau trouver la faille : à la suite d'une combinaison avec Noah Fontaine, Hugo Durin était trouvé dans un petit espace et sa frappe croisée du gauche faisait mouche. (ASSE-AFCA : 3-0, 24ème).Dès la remise en jeu, le défenseur central Idris Balta s'avançait, plaçait un tir qui rebondissait devant le but et trompait le gardien adverse. (ASSE-AFCA : 4-0, 25ème).A la 30ème minute, suite à un centre de Raphaël Moser, Hugo Durin remisait sur Joris Rochette (photo), mais la reprise de ce dernier était contrée. Aurillac réagissait timidement à la 38ème et sur un tir venu de loin, Ilan Clapeyron se détendait bien. C'était un petit intermède au milieu de la domination stéphanoise qui ne se démentait pas : un coup-franc de Rayan Fontaine trouvait la tête de Dixon Ahamadi, mais le gardien adverse captait le cuir (40ème), une frappe lointaine de Joris Rochette s'écrasait sur l'équerre du but cantalou (43ème), sur une combinaison côté gauche, le tir de Raphaël Moser dans un angle fermé frôlait la transversale (44ème), et, enfin à la 45ème, un tir dévié de Joris Rochette, manquait de tromper le gardien. La mi-temps était sifflée sur ces entrefaites. Le score de 4 à 0 illustrait la mainmise stéphanoise.

Le second acte repartait avec la même physionomie. A la 50ème une subtile combinaison Joris Rochette - Hugo Durin butait sur le gardien. A la 52ème, Joris Rochette repiquait au centre et sa frappe du gauche passait à côté. La minute suivante Hugo Durin alertait Rayan Fontaine et le centre de ce dernier trouvait la tête de son frère Noah, mais sa reprise était détournée en corner, corner sur lequel la tête de Dixon Ahamadi ne trouvait pas le cadre. A la 55ème, Rayan Fontaine était fauché à l'entrée de la surface et le coup-franc de Raphaël Moser s'écrasait sur la transversale aurillacoise. A force d'appuyer, les Verts allaient trouver la faille : une frappe de 25 mètres de Dixon Ahamadi était détournée par le gardien et sur le corner qui suivait Rayan Fontaine trouvait la tête d'Hugo Durin. (ASSE-AFCA : 5-0, 56ème) A la 62ème, Hugo Durin servait latéralement Lenny Nebout mais la frappe de ce dernier était stoppée par le gardien. Servi par une passe verticale d'Idris Balta, Kaïs Boukerma voyait son tir détourné par le portier aurillacois (65ème). A la 69ème, Rayan Fontaine trouvait Hugo Durin dans le dos de la défense et ce dernier concluait du droit. (ASSE-AFCA : 6-0, 69ème).Peu après, Hugo Durin, alerté par Joris Rochette, partait de la ligne médiane, prenait de vitesse la défense adverse et s'en allait marquer du droit. (ASSE-AFCA : 7-0, 72ème).Joris Rochette faisait un festival côté droit et son centre en retrait trouvait Riad Moutae qui  parachevait cette belle action. (ASSE-AFCA : 8-0, 76ème).Enfin, alors qu'il restait une dizaine de minutes à jouer, une belle passe cachée de Lenny Nebout pour Hugo Durin permettait à ce dernier de conclure d'une frappe décroisée entre le gardien et son poteau. C'était son 6ème but de l'après-midi ! (ASSE-AFCA : 9-0, 81ème). Le match à sens unique se terminait sur ce score fleuve qui reflétait bien la sérieuse prestation des jeunes Verts.

La composition : Clapeyron - R.Fontaine, Ahamadi, Balta, Moser - Evaristo, Rochette, Moussaoui (Boukerma, 60ème)- Nebout, Durin, N.Fontaine (Moutae, 60ème).

La réaction de Kevin de Jesus : "Satisfait du match ? Certes, mais mon but c'est de voir des comportements qui progressent, qui avancent vers ce que j'exige des jeunes. A la mi-temps, le résultat semblait acquis, mais j'ai demandé à mes joueurs de continuer à trouver des solutions pour déséquilibrer l'équipe adverse, de varier les combinaisons pour arriver à cet objectif, en diversifiant les circuits, en impliquant toute l'équipe dans cette recherche, défense y compris, par les montées des excentrés dans leur couloir, des centraux dans l'axe pour tenter des frappes de loin. J'ai apprécié le sérieux des joueurs qui sont restés concentrés, sans s'enflammer, en respectant l'adversaire. Cet esprit-là est important."
